\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md183}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{1. Introduction}{1. Introduction}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md183}
Educational institutions worldwide face one of the most complex logistical challenges in academic management:\+ creating a conflict-\/free weekly timetable. This task involves satisfying multiple simultaneous constraints including teacher availability, classroom capacity, course requirements, and departmental sectioning.

Manual timetable generation is not only time-\/consuming but also highly error-\/prone. A single scheduling conflict—two classes in the same room at the same time, or a teacher assigned two lectures simultaneously—can disrupt an entire department\textquotesingle{}s academic schedule for the whole semester.

The {\bfseries{Intelligent Timetable Management System (ITMS)}} addresses this challenge by providing a fully automated, web-\/based academic scheduling platform. Built on modern Python frameworks, it leverages constraint-\/satisfaction principles to generate clash-\/free timetables for multiple departments in a matter of seconds.

This comprehensive documentation provides a complete technical reference for the system, covering its architecture, data models, algorithms, user roles, API routes, frontend design, and deployment procedures. It is intended for developers, system administrators, and academic staff who will use or maintain ITMS.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md184}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{Key Highlights}{Key Highlights}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md184}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Automated Conflict-\/\+Free Scheduling}}:\+ Generates complete weekly timetables respecting all institutional constraints
\item {\bfseries{Multi-\/\+Course Type Support}}:\+ Handles Theory, Practical (Lab), and Activity classes with specific rules
\item {\bfseries{Role-\/\+Based Access Control}}:\+ Three-\/tier system for Admin, Teacher, and Student roles
\item {\bfseries{Bulk Data Import}}:\+ CSV upload support for rapid institutional setup
\item {\bfseries{Leave Management}}:\+ Teacher leave requests with automatic substitution assignment
\item {\bfseries{Internal Messaging}}:\+ Complete mailbox system for official communications
\item {\bfseries{PDF Export}}:\+ High-\/quality timetable exports for printing and sharing
\item {\bfseries{Modern UI}}:\+ Glassmorphism design with interactive particle animations
\item {\bfseries{Desktop Deployment}}:\+ Py\+Installer packaging for institutions without server infrastructure
\end{DoxyItemize}

\DoxyHorRuler{0}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md186}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{2. Project Overview}{2. Project Overview}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md186}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md187}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{2.\+1 Project Name}{2.\+1 Project Name}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md187}
{\bfseries{Intelligent Timetable Management System (ITMS)}}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md188}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{2.\+2 Project Description}{2.\+2 Project Description}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md188}
ITMS is an advanced, AI-\/driven academic scheduling solution built with Flask and SQLAlchemy. The system automates the generation of weekly timetables for college departments while respecting real-\/world constraints:\+


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Teacher workload limits (configurable per teacher)
\item Classroom type matching (Lab rooms for Practicals, regular for Theory)
\item Parallel batch handling for practical sessions
\item Activity class period assignment (last slot of the day)
\item Department section and semester differentiation
\end{DoxyItemize}

The system is accessible through any modern web browser and provides personalized dashboards for each user type. The administrator controls all data entry, timetable generation, and user management. Teachers can view their personal schedules and request adjustments. Students have a clean, read-\/only view of their department timetable.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md189}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{2.\+3 Project Goals}{2.\+3 Project Goals}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md189}
The system was designed around three primary goals:\+


\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item {\bfseries{AUTOMATION}} -\/ Remove the burden of manual scheduling from academic staff, replacing hours of work with a single click.
\item {\bfseries{ACCURACY}} -\/ Eliminate scheduling conflicts through multi-\/dimensional constraint checking across teachers, rooms, departments.
\item {\bfseries{ACCESSIBILITY}} -\/ Provide a modern web interface accessible to all stakeholders:\+ admin, teachers, and students.
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md190}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{2.\+4 Target Users}{2.\+4 Target Users}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md190}

\begin{DoxyTable}{2}{}{}{1}
\SetCell{c,bg=\tableheadbgcolor,font=\bfseries}User Role  &\SetCell{c,bg=\tableheadbgcolor,font=\bfseries}Primary Use Case  \\
{\bfseries{Admin}}  &Full system control:\+ data entry, timetable generation, user management, reporting, messaging  \\
{\bfseries{Teacher}}  &View personal schedule, edit their own sessions only, request leave, read inbox messages  \\
{\bfseries{Student}}  &Read-\/only access to their department timetable, view admin announcements  \\
\end{DoxyTable}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md191}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{2.\+5 System Requirements}{2.\+5 System Requirements}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md191}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md192}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Hardware Requirements}{Hardware Requirements}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md192}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Minimum}}:\+ 2GB RAM, 1GB storage, modern web browser
\item {\bfseries{Recommended}}:\+ 4GB RAM, 5GB storage, SSD storage for better performance
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md193}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Software Requirements}{Software Requirements}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md193}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Operating System}}:\+ Windows 10+, mac\+OS 12+, Ubuntu 20.\+04+
\item {\bfseries{Python}}:\+ Version 3.\+10 or higher
\item {\bfseries{Web Browser}}:\+ Chrome 90+, Firefox 88+, Edge 90+, Safari 14+
\end{DoxyItemize}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md195}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{3. Objectives of the System}{3. Objectives of the System}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md195}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md196}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{3.\+1 Primary Objectives}{3.\+1 Primary Objectives}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md196}

\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item {\bfseries{Intelligent Scheduling Algorithm}}:\+ Design and implement an automated scheduling algorithm capable of generating weekly timetables free from teacher, classroom, and department-\/level conflicts.
\item {\bfseries{Multi-\/\+Course Type Support}}:\+ Support multiple course types including Theory, Practical (Lab), and Activity classes, each with their own scheduling constraints and rules.
\item {\bfseries{Role-\/\+Based Access Control}}:\+ Implement a robust RBAC system so that each user type has access only to features relevant to their function.
\item {\bfseries{Bulk Data Management}}:\+ Allow administrators to bulk-\/import institutional data via CSV files, avoiding time-\/consuming manual data entry.
\item {\bfseries{Leave and Substitution Workflow}}:\+ Enable teachers to request leaves with automatic substitution management to ensure no class period goes unattended.
\item {\bfseries{Modern Web Interface}}:\+ Provide a contemporary web interface with PDF export capabilities for easy timetable printing and digital sharing.
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md197}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{3.\+2 Secondary Objectives}{3.\+2 Secondary Objectives}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md197}

\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item {\bfseries{Internal Communication System}}:\+ Provide an internal messaging system for official communication between the admin and teaching/\+student community.
\item {\bfseries{Desktop Application Support}}:\+ Build a deployable desktop application version using Py\+Installer and Py\+Web\+View for institutions without server infrastructure.
\item {\bfseries{Multi-\/\+Section Support}}:\+ Ensure the system correctly handles multi-\/section and multi-\/semester department configurations.
\item {\bfseries{Manual Adjustment Capabilities}}:\+ Allow manual timetable adjustments with real-\/time conflict detection and smart alternative slot suggestions.
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md198}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{3.\+3 Success Metrics}{3.\+3 Success Metrics}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md198}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Scheduling Success Rate}}:\+ 100\% conflict-\/free timetable generation for properly configured data
\item {\bfseries{User Adoption}}:\+ Intuitive interface requiring minimal training
\item {\bfseries{Performance}}:\+ Timetable generation completed in under 30 seconds for typical institutional data
\item {\bfseries{Data Integrity}}:\+ Zero data loss or corruption during operations
\item {\bfseries{Security}}:\+ Role-\/based access control preventing unauthorized data access
\end{DoxyItemize}

\begin{DoxyItemize}

\item Automated timetable generation with comprehensive conflict checking
\item Manual timetable entry editing with real-\/time conflict validation
\item Smart alternative slot suggestions during conflict detection
\item Teacher leave request workflow (Pending → Approved/\+\+Rejected)
\item Automatic substitution assignment on leave approval
\item Internal messaging system (broadcast and individual messages)
\item PDF export of complete and per-\/department timetables
\item CSV bulk import for all data categories
\item User registration for teachers and students
\item Profile management with password update capability
\item Custom 404 and 500 error handling pages
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md203}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Technical Scope}{Technical Scope}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md203}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Web-\/based application with responsive design
\item SQLite database with SQLAlchemy ORM
\item Flask web framework with Blueprint architecture
\item Role-\/based access control with Flask-\/\+Login
\item Client-\/side PDF generation using html2pdf.\+js
\item Desktop application packaging with Py\+Installer
\item Interactive particle background animation
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md204}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{4.\+2 Out of Scope}{4.\+2 Out of Scope}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md204}

\begin{DoxyItemize}

\item Mobile native application (Android/\+i\+OS)
\item Integration with external LMS (Moodle, Blackboard, Canvas)
\item Automated email or SMS notifications
\item AI-\/based teacher workload prediction
\item Attendance tracking module
\item Integration with calendar systems (Google Calendar, Outlook)
\item Multi-\/language support (i18n)
\item Theme customization options
\item Advanced reporting and analytics
\item API access for third-\/party integrations

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md206}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Assumptions}{Assumptions}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md206}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Institutions have basic computer literacy among staff
\item Internet connectivity is available for web-\/based access
\item CSV data provided by institutions is properly formatted
\item Maximum 6 working days per week (Monday-\/\+Saturday)
\item Maximum 7 time periods per day
\item Reasonable data volumes (hundreds of courses, not thousands)
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md207}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Constraints}{Constraints}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md207}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Single administrator account limitation
\item SQLite database (no concurrent multi-\/user support)
\item No real-\/time synchronization across multiple devices
\item Client-\/side PDF generation (limited by browser capabilities)
\item No offline functionality for web version
\end{DoxyItemize}

\item {\bfseries{Ecosystem}}:\+ Flask-\/\+Login and Flask-\/\+SQLAlchemy integrate seamlessly
\item {\bfseries{Deployment Options}}:\+ Can be packaged as desktop app via Py\+Web\+View + Py\+Installer
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md215}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{SQLite Selection Rationale}{SQLite Selection Rationale}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md215}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Zero Configuration}}:\+ No separate server process or installation required
\item {\bfseries{File-\/\+Based}}:\+ Makes the entire application fully portable and self-\/contained
\item {\bfseries{SQLAlchemy Compatible}}:\+ Fully supported by the chosen ORM
\item {\bfseries{Sufficient Scale}}:\+ Adequate for institutional use at the department level (hundreds to thousands of records)
\item {\bfseries{ACID Compliance}}:\+ Ensures data integrity and transactional safety
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md216}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Vanilla CSS Rationale}{Vanilla CSS Rationale}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md216}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Performance}}:\+ No framework overhead or unused styles
\item {\bfseries{Customization}}:\+ Complete design control for unique glassmorphism aesthetic
\item {\bfseries{Browser Support}}:\+ Works across all modern browsers without transpilation
\item {\bfseries{Maintainability}}:\+ Single CSS file with organized sections
\end{DoxyItemize}

\DoxyHorRuler{0}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md218}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{6. System Architecture}{6. System Architecture}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md218}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md219}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{6.\+1 Architectural Pattern}{6.\+1 Architectural Pattern}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md219}
The system follows the {\bfseries{Model-\/\+View-\/\+Controller (MVC)}} architectural pattern, implemented through Flask\textquotesingle{}s Blueprint and Jinja2 templating system:\+


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{MODEL}} → {\ttfamily models.\+py} (SQLAlchemy ORM classes and database schema)
\item {\bfseries{VIEW}} → {\ttfamily templates/\+} (Jinja2 HTML templates)
\item {\bfseries{CONTROLLER}} → {\ttfamily routes.\+py} (Flask route handlers and business logic)
\item {\bfseries{LOGIC}} → {\ttfamily scheduler.\+py} (Scheduling algorithm, separate concern)
\item {\bfseries{ENTRY}} → {\ttfamily app.\+py} (Application factory and configuration)
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md220}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{6.\+2 Architecture Diagram}{6.\+2 Architecture Diagram}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md220}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md223}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Request Flow Example}{Request Flow Example}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md223}

\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item {\bfseries{User Action}}:\+ Admin clicks "{}\+Generate Timetable"{} button
\item {\bfseries{HTTP Request}}:\+ {\ttfamily POST /\+generate} sent to Flask server
\item {\bfseries{Route Handler}}:\+ {\ttfamily routes.\+py:\+generate()} function executes
\item {\bfseries{Business Logic}}:\+ Calls {\ttfamily Scheduler.\+generate\+\_\+timetable()}
\item {\bfseries{Database Operations}}:\+ Scheduler queries models, creates Timetable\+Entry records
\item {\bfseries{Response}}:\+ Redirect to timetable page with success message
\item {\bfseries{Template Rendering}}:\+ {\ttfamily timetable.\+html} displays generated schedule
\item {\bfseries{Client-\/\+Side}}:\+ Java\+Script enhances UI with animations
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md224}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Data Flow Example}{Data Flow Example}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md224}

\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item {\bfseries{Input}}:\+ CSV file uploaded via {\ttfamily /\+upload\+\_\+csv}
\item {\bfseries{Parsing}}:\+ {\ttfamily routes.\+py} processes CSV with Python\textquotesingle{}s {\ttfamily csv.\+Dict\+Reader}
\item {\bfseries{Validation}}:\+ Checks foreign key relationships exist
\item {\bfseries{Database}}:\+ Creates model instances and commits transaction
\item {\bfseries{Feedback}}:\+ Success/\+error messages displayed to user
\end{DoxyEnumerate}

\DoxyHorRuler{0}

The system persists all data in a SQLite database named {\ttfamily timetable.\+db}. The database is managed entirely through SQLAlchemy ORM. The schema consists of 9 main tables representing the core entities of the system.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md228}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{7.\+2 Entity Relationship Diagram}{7.\+2 Entity Relationship Diagram}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md228}

The system supports 6 working days (Monday through Saturday) and 7 daily time periods. Period 7 (03:\+40-\/04:\+30) is reserved for Activity Classes. Periods 5 and 6 follow a lunch break (12:\+30 PM to 2:\+00 PM).\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md230}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{7.\+4 Data Model:\+ Department}{7.\+4 Data Model:\+ Department}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md230}

Course Types:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Theory}}:\+ Regular lecture (1 hour per slot, 1-\/6 hours/\+week)
\item {\bfseries{Practical}}:\+ Lab session (3 consecutive hours per slot)
\item {\bfseries{Activity Class}}:\+ Special period (always in Period 7, last slot)
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md232}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{7.\+6 Data Model:\+ Teacher}{7.\+6 Data Model:\+ Teacher}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md232}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md244}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{8.\+3 Key Sections Explained}{8.\+3 Key Sections Explained}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md244}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md245}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{8.\+3.\+1 Desktop vs. Development Mode}{8.\+3.\+1 Desktop vs. Development Mode}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md245}
The application detects whether it is running as a packaged .exe file (via Py\+Installer) using {\ttfamily sys.\+frozen}. In desktop mode, the SQLite database is stored in the user\textquotesingle{}s App\+Data folder for persistence. In development mode, the database is stored in the project\textquotesingle{}s {\ttfamily instance/\+} folder.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md246}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{8.\+3.\+2 Login\+Manager Configuration}{8.\+3.\+2 Login\+Manager Configuration}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md246}
Flask-\/\+Login is initialized with:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\ttfamily login\+\_\+view = \textquotesingle{}main.\+login\textquotesingle{}} → Redirects unauthenticated users to login
\item {\ttfamily user\+\_\+loader} callback → Loads user from DB by session {\ttfamily user\+\_\+id}
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md247}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{8.\+3.\+3 Context Processor:\+ inject\+\_\+unread\+\_\+count}{8.\+3.\+3 Context Processor:\+ inject\+\_\+unread\+\_\+count}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md247}
This function runs on every page render and injects the unread message count into all Jinja2 templates, allowing the navbar to display a notification badge without repeating the query in every route.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md248}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{8.\+3.\+4 Error Handlers}{8.\+3.\+4 Error Handlers}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md248}
Custom error pages are registered for HTTP 404 (Not Found) and HTTP 500 (Internal Server Error) to provide friendly error messages instead of default Flask error pages.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md249}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{8.\+3.\+5 Blueprint Registration}{8.\+3.\+5 Blueprint Registration}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md249}
All routes are organized into a Blueprint named \textquotesingle{}main\textquotesingle{} (defined in {\ttfamily routes.\+py}) and registered with the app using {\ttfamily app.\+register\+\_\+blueprint(main)}.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md250}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{8.\+3.\+6 Database Initialization}{8.\+3.\+6 Database Initialization}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md250}
{\ttfamily db.\+create\+\_\+all()} is called inside the app context to create all database tables on first run if they do not already exist.

\DoxyHorRuler{0}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md252}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{9. Routing and Controller Logic (routes.\+py)}{9. Routing and Controller Logic (routes.\+py)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md252}

{\ttfamily routes.\+py} is the largest file in the project (1055 lines). It contains all Flask route handlers (view functions) organized as a Blueprint. This file acts as the Controller layer:\+ it receives HTTP requests, performs business logic, queries the database, and returns rendered HTML responses.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md254}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{9.\+2 Blueprint Definition}{9.\+2 Blueprint Definition}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md254}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md267}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{10. Intelligent Scheduling Engine (scheduler.\+py)}{10. Intelligent Scheduling Engine (scheduler.\+py)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md267}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md268}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{10.\+1 Overview}{10.\+1 Overview}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md268}
The Scheduler class in {\ttfamily scheduler.\+py} is the heart of the system. It implements a constraint-\/satisfaction-\/based greedy scheduling algorithm that generates a complete weekly timetable by assigning courses to available time slots while respecting all institutional constraints.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md269}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{10.\+2 Scheduling Algorithm Design}{10.\+2 Scheduling Algorithm Design}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md269}
The algorithm follows a {\bfseries{priority-\/based greedy approach}}:\+\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md270}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Step 1:\+ REQUIREMENT COLLECTION}{Step 1:\+ REQUIREMENT COLLECTION}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md270}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Query all departments and their courses
\item For each course, fetch assigned teacher from Allocations
\item Build a flat list of "{}requirements"{} (one per scheduled session)
\item Practical courses generate 3-\/hour block requirements
\item Theory courses generate 1-\/hour requirements per {\ttfamily hours\+\_\+per\+\_\+week}
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md271}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Step 2:\+ PRIORITIZATION}{Step 2:\+ PRIORITIZATION}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md271}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Sort requirements:\+ Practicals (duration=3) scheduled FIRST
\item This ensures lab blocks get their slots before theory fills them
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md272}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Step 3:\+ DOMAIN BUILDING}{Step 3:\+ DOMAIN BUILDING}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md272}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Practicals:\+ only allowed to start at Period 2 (10:\+50) or Period 5 (2:\+00)
\item Theory:\+ may use Periods 1-\/6; Period 7 is reserved for Activity Classes
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md273}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Step 4:\+ CONSTRAINT CHECKING (for each candidate slot)}{Step 4:\+ CONSTRAINT CHECKING (for each candidate slot)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md273}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Teacher Conflict}}:\+ teacher not teaching another class at this time
\item {\bfseries{Room Conflict}}:\+ classroom not already occupied at this time
\item {\bfseries{Department Conflict}}:\+ department not exceeding concurrency limit
\item {\bfseries{Lab Block Rule}}:\+ only one lab start time per department per day
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md274}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Step 5:\+ ROOM MATCHING}{Step 5:\+ ROOM MATCHING}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md274}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Practical sessions must use rooms of type \textquotesingle{}Lab\textquotesingle{}
\item Theory sessions must use rooms of type \textquotesingle{}Classroom\textquotesingle{}
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md275}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Step 6:\+ ACTIVITY CLASS ASSIGNMENT}{Step 6:\+ ACTIVITY CLASS ASSIGNMENT}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md275}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item After all main classes are placed, Activity Classes are scheduled
\item Activity Classes always go to Period 7 (03:\+40-\/04:\+30)
\item The assigned teacher is inherited from the Period 6 teacher on that day
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md276}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Step 7:\+ DATABASE COMMIT}{Step 7:\+ DATABASE COMMIT}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md276}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Delete existing timetable entries
\item Bulk-\/insert all generated schedule entries
\item Rollback on any database error
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md277}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{10.\+3 Scheduler Class Constructor}{10.\+3 Scheduler Class Constructor}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md277}

\begin{DoxyTable}{2}{}{}{1}
\SetCell{c,bg=\tableheadbgcolor,font=\bfseries}Constraint  &\SetCell{c,bg=\tableheadbgcolor,font=\bfseries}Description  \\
Teacher uniqueness  &One teacher in one place at a time  \\
Room uniqueness  &One class per room per time slot  \\
Dept. concurrency limit  &Max 2 parallel sessions per department  \\
Lab start restriction  &Labs start only at Period 2 or Period 5  \\
One lab block per day  &One lab block start time per dept/\+day  \\
Same subject distribution  &Same subject max 2 sessions per day  \\
Activity Class slot  &Always Period 7, inherit Period 6 teacher  \\
Room type matching  &Labs→\+Lab rooms $|$ Theory→\+Classrooms  \\
\end{DoxyTable}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md282}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{11. Role-\/\+Based Access Control (RBAC)}{11. Role-\/\+Based Access Control (RBAC)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md282}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md283}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{11.\+1 Overview}{11.\+1 Overview}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md283}
The system implements a three-\/tier Role-\/\+Based Access Control (RBAC) model. Each user account has a \textquotesingle{}role\textquotesingle{} field that determines what actions they can perform and what pages they can access.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md284}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{11.\+2 Role Definitions}{11.\+2 Role Definitions}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md284}

\begin{DoxyTable}{2}{}{}{1}
\SetCell{c,bg=\tableheadbgcolor,font=\bfseries}Role  &\SetCell{c,bg=\tableheadbgcolor,font=\bfseries}Permissions  \\
{\bfseries{admin}}  &Full system access. Can create/\+edit/\+delete all entities, generate timetables, manage users, send messages, approve leaves, and access CSV upload features.  \\
{\bfseries{teacher}}  &View personal timetable, edit their own sessions only, submit leave requests, view inbox messages.  \\
{\bfseries{student}}  &Read-\/only access to their department timetable, view admin announcements.  \\
\end{DoxyTable}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md285}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{11.\+3 Access Control Implementation}{11.\+3 Access Control Implementation}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md285}
Access control is enforced at three levels:\+\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md286}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Level 1 -\/ Route Decorators}{Level 1 -\/ Route Decorators}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md286}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\ttfamily @login\+\_\+required} → Ensures user is authenticated (any role)
\item {\ttfamily @admin\+\_\+required} → Ensures user has \textquotesingle{}admin\textquotesingle{} role
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md287}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Level 2 -\/ In-\/route Role Checks}{Level 2 -\/ In-\/route Role Checks}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md287}

\begin{DoxyCode}{0}

\DoxyCodeLine{\ \ \ \ flash(\textcolor{stringliteral}{'Access\ denied.'})}
\DoxyCodeLine{\ \ \ \ redirect(...)}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md291}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{12. User Interface and Frontend Design}{12. User Interface and Frontend Design}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md291}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md292}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{12.\+1 Design Philosophy}{12.\+1 Design Philosophy}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md292}
The system uses a modern {\bfseries{Glassmorphism}} design language characterized by:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Frosted glass effect using CSS {\ttfamily backdrop-\/filter:\+ blur()}
\item Semi-\/transparent cards with {\ttfamily rgba()} backgrounds
\item Glowing neon accents in blue and purple tones
\item Smooth CSS transitions and hover animations
\item HTML5 Canvas particle network background (animated)
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md293}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{12.\+2 Master Layout (base.\+html)}{12.\+2 Master Layout (base.\+html)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md293}
All pages extend a single {\ttfamily base.\+html} master template which provides:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Navigation bar with role-\/aware menu items
\item Unread message notification badge (injected by context processor)
\item Particle network canvas background (runs on all pages)
\item Flash message alert display (success/\+danger/\+warning)
\item Footer with system version info
\item CSS and JS block sections for page-\/specific resources
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md294}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{12.\+3 Key Pages}{12.\+3 Key Pages}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md294}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md295}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{LOGIN PAGE (login.\+html)}{LOGIN PAGE (login.\+html)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md295}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Centered glassmorphism card on animated particle background
\item Username and password input fields
\item Flash message area for login failure alerts
\item Link to registration page
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md296}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DASHBOARD (dashboard.\+html)}{DASHBOARD (dashboard.\+html)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md296}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Statistics cards showing counts of Departments, Teachers, Courses, Rooms
\item Quick action buttons (Generate Timetable, Upload CSV)
\item Role-\/aware content:\+ admin sees management buttons, teachers see schedule
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md297}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{TIMETABLE PAGE (timetable.\+html)}{TIMETABLE PAGE (timetable.\+html)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md297}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Master timetable view for all departments (admin/\+student view)
\item Personal timetable view for teacher role
\item Colour-\/coded cells:\+ Theory (blue), Practical (green), Activity (orange)
\item Colspan merging for multi-\/period Lab blocks (3 cells merged into 1)
\item Today\textquotesingle{}s date is highlighted on the current day column
\item Substitution teacher names shown in real-\/time when applicable
\item Department filter dropdown to focus on specific branch
\item PDF Export button (triggers html2pdf.\+js client-\/side rendering)
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md298}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{EDIT TIMETABLE ENTRY (edit\+\_\+timetable\+\_\+entry.\+html)}{EDIT TIMETABLE ENTRY (edit\+\_\+timetable\+\_\+entry.\+html)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md298}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Dropdown selectors for Day, Timeslot, Classroom, Teacher
\item Real-\/time conflict alert displayed on submission failure
\item Suggested alternative slots listed with one-\/click application
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md299}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{12.\+4 Timetable Grid Structure}{12.\+4 Timetable Grid Structure}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md299}

\begin{DoxyItemize}

\end{DoxyItemize}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md302}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{13. CSV Bulk Import Feature}{13. CSV Bulk Import Feature}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md302}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md303}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{13.\+1 Overview}{13.\+1 Overview}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md303}
To allow institutions to set up the system rapidly, ITMS supports bulk data import via CSV (Comma-\/\+Separated Values) files. This allows administrators to upload hundreds of teachers, courses, and classrooms simultaneously rather than entering them one by one manually.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md304}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{13.\+2 Supported Import Types}{13.\+2 Supported Import Types}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md304}

\begin{DoxyTable}{3}{}{}{1}
\SetCell{c,bg=\tableheadbgcolor,font=\bfseries}Type  &\SetCell{c,bg=\tableheadbgcolor,font=\bfseries}Required CSV Columns  &\SetCell{c,bg=\tableheadbgcolor,font=\bfseries}Notes  \\
departments  &Name, Code, Section, Semester  &\\
teachers  &Teacher\+Name, Department\+Code, Email, Max\+Hours\+Per\+Week  &Dept code must exist  \\
subjects  &Name, Code, Department\+Code, Hours\+Per\+Week, Type  &\\
classrooms  &Name, Capacity, Type  &\\
allocations  &Course\+Code, Teacher\+Name  &Both must exist  \\
\end{DoxyTable}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md308}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{14. Leave Management and Substitution System}{14. Leave Management and Substitution System}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md308}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md309}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{14.\+1 Overview}{14.\+1 Overview}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md309}
The system includes a complete leave management workflow allowing teachers to submit leave requests and admins to approve or reject them. When a leave is approved, the system automatically finds substitute teachers for all affected classes and creates substitution records. Those substitutions are displayed on the timetable page to all users on the specific date.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md310}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{14.\+2 Leave Request Workflow}{14.\+2 Leave Request Workflow}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md310}

\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item {\bfseries{Teacher submits leave request}} via {\ttfamily /\+leave\+\_\+request} route
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Selects date and provides reason
\item Status is set to \textquotesingle{}Pending\textquotesingle{}
\end{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Admin reviews pending requests}} via {\ttfamily /\+admin/\+leaves} route
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Sees all pending requests from all teachers
\item Can approve or reject with optional admin feedback
\end{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{On APPROVAL}}:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item System queries the leaving teacher\textquotesingle{}s timetable for that date
\item For each affected Timetable\+Entry, finds an available substitute
\item Creates Substitution records linking leave → entry → sub teacher
\item Auto-\/sends a Message to the substitute teacher notifying them
\end{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{On the leave date}}:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\ttfamily /\+timetable} route queries active substitutions for today
\item Displays substitute teacher names in affected cells
\item All users can see the substitution info in real-\/time
\end{DoxyItemize}
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md311}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{14.\+3 Leave\+Request States}{14.\+3 Leave\+Request States}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md311}

\begin{DoxyTable}{2}{}{}{1}
\SetCell{c,bg=\tableheadbgcolor,font=\bfseries}Status  &\SetCell{c,bg=\tableheadbgcolor,font=\bfseries}Meaning  \\
Pending  &Submitted by teacher, awaiting admin review  \\
Approved  &Admin approved; substitutions auto-\/created  \\
Rejected  &Admin rejected; no substitutions created  \\
\end{DoxyTable}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md312}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{14.\+4 Substitution Display Logic}{14.\+4 Substitution Display Logic}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md312}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md314}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{15. Internal Messaging (Mailbox) System}{15. Internal Messaging (Mailbox) System}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md314}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md315}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{15.\+1 Overview}{15.\+1 Overview}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md315}
The system includes a complete internal messaging module allowing administrators to send individual or broadcast messages to users. Every user has a personal inbox (mailbox) where they can read messages and mark them as read.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md316}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{15.\+2 Messaging Features}{15.\+2 Messaging Features}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md316}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Admin can send messages to:\+ individual users, all teachers, all students, or everyone on the system
\item System auto-\/sends messages for:\+ leave approvals, leave rejections, and substitution notifications
\item Unread message count shown as badge in navigation bar
\item Full message thread view with timestamp, sender, subject, body
\item Bulk clear mailbox (delete all messages at once)
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md317}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{15.\+3 Compose Message Route}{15.\+3 Compose Message Route}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md317}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md321}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{16. PDF Export Feature}{16. PDF Export Feature}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md321}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md322}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{16.\+1 Overview}{16.\+1 Overview}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md322}
The system provides a one-\/click PDF export feature allowing users to download a high-\/quality PDF copy of the timetable directly from the web interface. The export is performed entirely on the client side using the html2pdf.\+js library, meaning no server processing is required.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md323}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{16.\+2 How It Works}{16.\+2 How It Works}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md323}

\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item The user clicks the "{}\+Export PDF"{} button on the timetable page.
\item Java\+Script code captures the specific HTML element containing the timetable grid using {\ttfamily document.\+get\+Element\+By\+Id()}.
\item html2pdf.\+js converts that HTML (including CSS styles) into a PDF.
\item The PDF is automatically downloaded to the user\textquotesingle{}s device.
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md324}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{16.\+3 PDF Export Java\+Script}{16.\+3 PDF Export Java\+Script}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md324}

\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{function\ exportPDF()\ \{}
\DoxyCodeLine{\ \ \ \ const\ element\ =\ document.getElementById('timetable-\/container');}
\DoxyCodeLine{\ \ \ \ const\ options\ =\ \{}
\DoxyCodeLine{\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ margin:\ 0.5,}
\DoxyCodeLine{\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ filename:\ 'timetable\_export.pdf',}
\DoxyCodeLine{\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ image:\ \{\ type:\ 'jpeg',\ quality:\ 0.98\ \},}
\DoxyCodeLine{\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ html2canvas:\ \{\ scale:\ 2,\ useCORS:\ true\ \},}
\DoxyCodeLine{\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ jsPDF:\ \{\ unit:\ 'in',\ format:\ 'a4',\ orientation:\ 'landscape'\ \}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\ \ \ \ \};}
\DoxyCodeLine{\ \ \ \ html2pdf().set(options).from(element).save();}
\DoxyCodeLine{\}}

\end{DoxyCode}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md325}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{16.\+4 Export Options}{16.\+4 Export Options}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md325}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Full Institution Timetable}}:\+ exports all department timetables on a single multi-\/page PDF document
\item {\bfseries{Per-\/\+Department PDF}}:\+ exports a filtered single-\/department timetable

\end{DoxyItemize}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md334}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{STEP 5:\+ Access the application}{STEP 5:\+ Access the application}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md334}
Open your web browser and go to:\+ 
\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{http://127.0.0.1:5000}

\end{DoxyCode}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md335}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{STEP 6:\+ First Admin Login}{STEP 6:\+ First Admin Login}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md335}
The system bootstraps automatically. Use:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Username:\+ {\ttfamily admin}
\item Password:\+ {\ttfamily admin}
\end{DoxyItemize}

On first login with these credentials, the admin account is created automatically in the database.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md336}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{17.\+3 Initial Setup Workflow (After Install)}{17.\+3 Initial Setup Workflow (After Install)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md336}

\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item {\bfseries{Add Departments}} → {\ttfamily /\+departments}
\item {\bfseries{Add Classrooms}} → {\ttfamily /\+classrooms}
\item {\bfseries{Add Teachers}} → {\ttfamily /\+teachers}
\item {\bfseries{Add Courses}} → {\ttfamily /\+courses}
\item {\bfseries{Assign Teachers}} → {\ttfamily /\+allocations} (link course to teacher)
\item {\bfseries{Generate Timetable}} → Dashboard → "{}\+Generate Timetable"{} button
\item {\bfseries{View Timetable}} → {\ttfamily /\+timetable}
\end{DoxyEnumerate}

Alternatively, Steps 1-\/5 can be done via CSV upload:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Dashboard → Upload CSV → Select type → Upload file
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md337}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{17.\+4 Desktop Application Build (Optional)}{17.\+4 Desktop Application Build (Optional)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md337}
To build a standalone Windows .exe (no Python required for end user):\+


\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{pyinstaller\ TimetableManager.spec}

\end{DoxyCode}


The {\ttfamily .spec} file is pre-\/configured in the project root. After build:\+ 
\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{dist/TimetableManager/TimetableManager.exe}

\end{DoxyCode}


The desktop app uses Py\+Web\+View to open the Flask server in a native OS window, providing an app-\/like experience without a browser.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md338}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{17.\+5 Resetting the Database}{17.\+5 Resetting the Database}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md338}
To reset all data and start fresh:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Delete the file:\+ {\ttfamily instance/\+timetable.\+db}
\item Restart the application:\+ {\ttfamily python app.\+py}
\item A fresh database will be auto-\/created on startup.
\end{DoxyItemize}

\DoxyHorRuler{0}

The system includes utility scripts for validating the generated timetable and verifying schedule density. These scripts run independently and output diagnostic information.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md342}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{18.\+2 Schedule Density Checker (check\+\_\+density.\+py)}{18.\+2 Schedule Density Checker (check\+\_\+density.\+py)}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md342}

{\itshape Description:\+ The dashboard displays system statistics in glassmorphism cards showing counts of departments, teachers, courses, and classrooms. Admin users see additional quick action buttons for timetable generation and CSV upload.}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md364}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{20.\+3 Timetable View}{20.\+3 Timetable View}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md364}

Lab sessions span multiple cells with colspan merging.}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md365}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{20.\+4 Edit Timetable Entry}{20.\+4 Edit Timetable Entry}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md365}
{\bfseries{\+[Screenshot:\+ Edit Form -\/ Dropdown selectors with conflict detection]\+}}

{\itshape Description:\+ The edit form provides dropdown selectors for day, timeslot, classroom, and teacher. The navigation bar displays an unread message count badge.}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md367}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{20.\+6 CSV Upload}{20.\+6 CSV Upload}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md367}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md372}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{21.\+1 Common Issues and Solutions}{21.\+1 Common Issues and Solutions}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md372}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md373}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Issue:\+ "{}\+Failed to generate timetable"{}}{Issue:\+ "{}\+Failed to generate timetable"{}}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md373}
{\bfseries{Symptoms:\+}} Clicking "{}\+Generate Timetable"{} shows error message {\bfseries{Causes:\+}}
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item No course-\/to-\/teacher allocations exist
\item Insufficient classrooms for the number of courses
\item Teacher workload limits exceeded {\bfseries{Solutions:\+}}
\end{DoxyItemize}
\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item Check allocations page -\/ ensure all courses have assigned teachers
\item Verify sufficient classrooms exist (especially labs for practical courses)
\item Review teacher workload limits in teacher profiles
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md374}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Issue:\+ "{}\+Invalid username or password"{}}{Issue:\+ "{}\+Invalid username or password"{}}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md374}
{\bfseries{Symptoms:\+}} Login fails with error message {\bfseries{Causes:\+}}
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Incorrect credentials
\item Database corruption
\item First-\/time setup not completed {\bfseries{Solutions:\+}}
\end{DoxyItemize}
\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item Try default admin credentials:\+ {\ttfamily admin} /\+ {\ttfamily admin}
\item Reset database by deleting {\ttfamily instance/\+timetable.\+db}
\item Check database file permissions
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md375}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Issue:\+ "{}\+Classroom is already occupied"{}}{Issue:\+ "{}\+Classroom is already occupied"{}}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md375}
{\bfseries{Symptoms:\+}} Conflict error when editing timetable entries {\bfseries{Causes:\+}}
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Manual scheduling conflict
\item Room double-\/booked {\bfseries{Solutions:\+}}
\end{DoxyItemize}
\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item Use the suggested alternative slots provided
\item Check existing timetable for the selected day/\+timeslot
\item Choose a different classroom or time
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md376}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Issue:\+ "{}\+Department batch limit reached"{}}{Issue:\+ "{}\+Department batch limit reached"{}}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md376}
{\bfseries{Symptoms:\+}} Cannot schedule more than 2 parallel sessions per department {\bfseries{Causes:\+}}
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Department concurrency limit exceeded
\item Too many courses scheduled simultaneously {\bfseries{Solutions:\+}}
\end{DoxyItemize}
\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item Review department course load
\item Spread courses across different timeslots
\item Consider adding more sections to the department
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md377}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Issue:\+ CSV import fails}{Issue:\+ CSV import fails}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md377}
{\bfseries{Symptoms:\+}} Upload succeeds but no data imported {\bfseries{Causes:\+}}
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Incorrect CSV format
\item Missing required columns
\item Foreign key references don\textquotesingle{}t exist {\bfseries{Solutions:\+}}
\end{DoxyItemize}
\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item Verify CSV format matches sample files
\item Ensure referenced departments/\+courses exist before importing allocations
\item Check for special characters in CSV data
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md378}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Issue:\+ PDF export is blank or corrupted}{Issue:\+ PDF export is blank or corrupted}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md378}
{\bfseries{Symptoms:\+}} Downloaded PDF is empty or malformed {\bfseries{Causes:\+}}
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Browser compatibility issues
\item Java\+Script disabled
\item Large timetable causing memory issues {\bfseries{Solutions:\+}}
\end{DoxyItemize}
\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item Use Chrome or Firefox browser
\item Enable Java\+Script in browser settings
\item Try exporting smaller department-\/specific timetables
\item Clear browser cache and retry
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md379}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Issue:\+ Application won\textquotesingle{}t start}{Issue:\+ Application won\textquotesingle{}t start}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md379}
{\bfseries{Symptoms:\+}} {\ttfamily python app.\+py} fails or shows import errors {\bfseries{Causes:\+}}
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Missing dependencies
\item Python version incompatibility
\item Corrupted virtual environment {\bfseries{Solutions:\+}}
\end{DoxyItemize}
\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item Recreate virtual environment:\+ {\ttfamily python -\/m venv venv}
\item Reinstall dependencies:\+ {\ttfamily pip install -\/r requirements.\+txt}
\item Verify Python version:\+ {\ttfamily python -\/-\/version} (should be 3.\+10+)
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md380}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Issue:\+ Database locked errors}{Issue:\+ Database locked errors}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md380}
{\bfseries{Symptoms:\+}} "{}\+Database is locked"{} error messages {\bfseries{Causes:\+}}
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Multiple application instances running
\item File permission issues
\item Antivirus software blocking database access {\bfseries{Solutions:\+}}
\end{DoxyItemize}
\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item Close all application instances
\item Check file permissions on {\ttfamily timetable.\+db}
\item Temporarily disable antivirus during development
\item Use database browser to check file integrity
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md381}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Issue:\+ Substitution not showing on timetable}{Issue:\+ Substitution not showing on timetable}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md381}
{\bfseries{Symptoms:\+}} Leave approved but no substitute teacher displayed {\bfseries{Causes:\+}}
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Substitution records not created
\item Date mismatch
\item Teacher availability issues during substitution assignment {\bfseries{Solutions:\+}}
\end{DoxyItemize}
\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item Check substitution table in database
\item Verify leave approval date matches current date
\item Review teacher availability on the leave date
\item Manually assign substitute if auto-\/assignment failed
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md382}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Issue:\+ Particle background not animating}{Issue:\+ Particle background not animating}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md382}
{\bfseries{Symptoms:\+}} Canvas background is static or blank {\bfseries{Causes:\+}}
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Java\+Script disabled
\item Canvas not supported
\item Performance issues on low-\/end devices {\bfseries{Solutions:\+}}
\end{DoxyItemize}
\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item Enable Java\+Script in browser
\item Use modern browser with Canvas support
\item Reduce particle count in Java\+Script for better performance
\item Background is non-\/essential -\/ application functions without it
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md383}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{21.\+2 Performance Optimization}{21.\+2 Performance Optimization}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md383}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md410}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{23.\+1 Database Performance}{23.\+1 Database Performance}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md410}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md411}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Indexing Strategy}{Indexing Strategy}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md411}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Primary keys automatically indexed by SQLite
\item Foreign key columns should be indexed for JOIN performance
\item Consider composite indexes for common query patterns
\end{DoxyItemize}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md412}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Query Optimization}{Query Optimization}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md412}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Use {\ttfamily select} for specific columns instead of loading full objects
\item Implement pagination for large result sets
\item Use {\ttfamily exists()} queries for boolean checks instead of {\ttfamily count()}
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md413}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Connection Management}{Connection Management}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md413}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item SQLite handles concurrent connections well for read operations
\item Write operations are serialized to prevent corruption
\item Connection pooling not needed for SQLite (file-\/based)
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md414}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{23.\+2 Application Performance}{23.\+2 Application Performance}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md414}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md415}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Caching Strategies}{Caching Strategies}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md415}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Browser caching for static assets (CSS, JS, images)
\item Database query result caching for frequently accessed data
\item Template fragment caching for complex rendered sections
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md416}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Memory Management}{Memory Management}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md416}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Clear large data structures after processing
\item Use generators for large dataset iteration
\item Implement lazy loading for related objects
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md417}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Response Time Optimization}{Response Time Optimization}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md417}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Keep database queries efficient (\texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} 100ms per request)
\item Minimize template rendering time
\item Compress responses for large HTML output
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md418}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{23.\+3 Scalability Considerations}{23.\+3 Scalability Considerations}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md418}
\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md419}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Data Volume Limits}{Data Volume Limits}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md419}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Tested with:\+ 10 departments, 50 teachers, 100 courses, 50 rooms
\item Recommended maximum:\+ 20 departments, 200 teachers, 500 courses
\item Performance degrades with \texorpdfstring{$>$}{>} 1000 timetable entries
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md420}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Concurrent Users}{Concurrent Users}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md420}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item SQLite supports multiple readers, single writer
\item Web application can handle 10-\/20 concurrent users
\item Desktop mode isolates database per user installation
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md421}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Hardware Requirements}{Hardware Requirements}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md421}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Minimum:\+ 2GB RAM, modern dual-\/core CPU
\item Recommended:\+ 4GB RAM, SSD storage
\item Database size:\+ \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} 50MB for medium institution
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md422}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{23.\+4 Monitoring and Profiling}{23.\+4 Monitoring and Profiling}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md422}

\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md465}{}\doxysection{\texorpdfstring{26. Conclusion}{26. Conclusion}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md465}
The Intelligent Timetable Management System (ITMS) successfully addresses one of the most persistent and complex administrative challenges faced by academic institutions:\+ the generation and management of a conflict-\/free weekly class schedule.

By leveraging the power of Python, Flask, and SQLAlchemy alongside a custom constraint-\/satisfaction scheduling algorithm, the system delivers a comprehensive, automated solution that eliminates manual scheduling effort and removes the risk of human error.\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md466}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{Key Achievements}{Key Achievements}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md466}

\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item {\bfseries{AUTOMATED SCHEDULING}}:\+ The Scheduler class generates complete, valid weekly timetables in seconds, enforcing all real-\/world constraints including teacher availability, room type matching, lab block rules, and department concurrency limits.
\item {\bfseries{ROLE-\/\+BASED SYSTEM}}:\+ The three-\/tier RBAC model (Admin, Teacher, Student) ensures every user interacts with only the features relevant to their function, maintaining data integrity and access control.
\item {\bfseries{COMPLETE WORKFLOW}}:\+ Beyond timetable generation, the system covers the full academic scheduling lifecycle — from initial data setup to leave management, substitution assignment, and official communications.
\item {\bfseries{MODERN INTERFACE}}:\+ The Glassmorphism UI with Canvas particle animations provides a polished, professional user experience that sets this application apart from traditional academic management tools.
\item {\bfseries{PORTABILITY}}:\+ The combination of SQLite (file-\/based database) and Py\+Installer packaging allows the system to be deployed as a zero-\/dependency desktop application for institutions without server access.
\end{DoxyEnumerate}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md467}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{Technical Excellence}{Technical Excellence}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md467}
The system demonstrates mastery of modern web development practices:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Clean Architecture}}:\+ MVC pattern with clear separation of concerns
\item {\bfseries{Database Design}}:\+ Normalized schema with proper relationships and constraints
\item {\bfseries{Algorithm Design}}:\+ Constraint satisfaction approach with backtracking prevention
\item {\bfseries{User Experience}}:\+ Intuitive interface with real-\/time feedback and validation
\item {\bfseries{Code Quality}}:\+ Well-\/structured, documented, and maintainable codebase
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md468}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{Educational Value}{Educational Value}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md468}
This project serves as an excellent case study for:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{Software Engineering}}:\+ Requirements analysis, system design, and implementation
\item {\bfseries{Database Design}}:\+ Entity relationships, normalization, and query optimization
\item {\bfseries{Algorithm Development}}:\+ Constraint satisfaction and heuristic optimization
\item {\bfseries{Web Development}}:\+ Full-\/stack application with modern UI/\+\+UX principles
\item {\bfseries{Project Management}}:\+ Version control, testing, and deployment strategies
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md469}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{Future Potential}{Future Potential}}\label{md__t_i_m_e_t_a_b_l_e___d_o_c_u_m_e_n_t_a_t_i_o_n_autotoc_md469}
The modular architecture and comprehensive feature set provide a solid foundation for future enhancements. The system can evolve from a departmental scheduling tool to a comprehensive campus management platform with attendance tracking, grade management, and advanced analytics.

This project not only solves a practical problem but also demonstrates the application of computer science principles to real-\/world challenges, making it an invaluable addition to any academic software engineering portfolio.
